00221: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
00222:   ymd,
00223:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00224:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
00225: ) {

00229:   return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL;
00230: }

00137: function isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(draw, lotteryKey) {
00138:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00139:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00140: 
00141:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
00142: 
00143:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00144:     lotteryKey,
00145:     ymd: y,
00146:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00147:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00148:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00149:   });
00150: 
00151:   const normalizedSchedule = Array.isArray(schedule)
00152:     ? schedule.map(toHourBucket).filter(Boolean)
00153:     : [];
00154: 
00155:   return normalizedSchedule.includes(h);
